- **ib_0dte**: Find and place the best 0DTE (zero-days-to-expiration) credit spreads from Interactive Brokers. Default execution route is the EMA9/EMA21 + VIX/VXN regime strategy (ema_vix_0dte.py), which auto-selects bull_put or bear_call from a bare EMA cross and skips the trade when the vol index is elevated (VXN ≥ 35 for NDX/QQQ, VIX ≥ 20 otherwise). Optional --rr-gate and --time-gate add red→red and bar-timing confirmation. Supports cash-settled indices (SPX, NDX, RUT, VIX) and any optionable stock/ETF. Manual spread type override is available via zero_dte.py. Requires TWS or IB Gateway running locally. ([ElevenZhou/codex-skills-library](https://github.com/ElevenZhou/codex-skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/ai%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%BA%93/%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96%E4%BA%A4%E6%98%93AI%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%BA%93/03_Skills/%E6%8C%89%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E7%B4%A2%E5%BC%95/%E6%9C%9F%E6%9D%83%E4%B8%8E%E8%A1%8D%E7%94%9F%E5%93%81/%E5%BD%93%E6%97%A5%E5%88%B0%E6%9C%9F%E4%BB%B7%E5%B7%AE))
